## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: milestones, launches, business development, the opportunity sizing on slides 11 to 13, then readouts and priorities. Action titles are mostly assertive, and the identity is consistent with navy header bands, a teal palette and a logo on each page. The key charts on slides 11 and 13 are clear. Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room. Slide 18 is an empty appendix divider, and slide 3 is a dense wall of legal text, which is expected here.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the slide 11 bar comparison with its >2X bracket and large figures, the stacked build on slide 13, and the consistent navy title band and logo system.

**Most useful repair:** Fill or rebalance the empty right side of slide 14, raise contrast on the pale labels on slide 19, and enlarge text on the slide 21 timeline and the slide 12 program grid.

**What would change the score:** I would split the slide 21 timeline across two pages and trim the cumulative greyed-out lists on slides 6 and 7 so each page shows one clear set of milestones.

_Confidence high. 22 of 22 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide for Merck's presentation at the JP Morgan Healthcare Conference, followed by a Business and Pipeline Update delivered by the Chairman and Chief Executive Officer, and a forward-looking statement disclaimer. It then moves through the company's business development activity, its 2025 approvals and trial milestones across oncology, cardiometabolic, infectious disease and animal health, and launch revenue for newer products. Next it sizes the commercial opportunity from new growth drivers against consensus KEYTRUDA sales, and walks through ten key programs and additional pipeline programs. A timeline slide lays out upcoming Phase 3 readouts through 2027. The closing section summarises the strategic priorities, followed by a Q&A slide and an appendix with updated opportunity estimates by therapeutic area and an acronym list. Slides 11, 12 and 19 are worth studying for how large numbers and pipeline categories are presented as simple visual comparisons.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Merck & Co., Inc. JP Morgan Healthcare Conference presentation have?**

The Merck & Co., Inc. deck has 22 slides. Slide 22 is the final slide, an acronym list that defines the abbreviations used in the presentation.

**What does the Merck & Co., Inc. deck say about the commercial opportunity from its new growth drivers?**

Slide 4 says the commercial opportunity from new growth drivers increased to >$70B, which is non-risk adjusted annual sales by the mid 2030s. Slide 11 compares this with consensus 2028 total KEYTRUDA sales of ~$35B, showing the new growth driver opportunity is more than double that figure.

**What does the Merck & Co., Inc. deck say about its ongoing Phase 3 studies?**

Slide 4 states that Merck & Co., Inc. has ~80 Phase 3 studies ongoing under its Advancing Diverse & Expansive Pipeline heading. The same slide says the company expects more than 20 new launches, almost all of which have blockbuster potential.

**What does the Merck & Co., Inc. deck say about the timing of upcoming Phase 3 readouts?**

Slide 16 says the company is anticipating multiple impactful Phase 3 readouts through 2027. Slide 14 lists 2026 readouts, including ISLEND-1 and ISLEND-2 with a PCD of Apr 2026 for islatravir and lenacapavir.

**Layout**

Two-column split with a full-height left column: a navy title panel occupies the upper left (0 to 40.6% of slide height) and a photograph fills the lower left (40.4% to 100%) edge to edge. The right column holds three icon-and-heading rows stacked at the top, a thin grey divider with a downward chevron in the middle, and a teal-accented conclusion row below, with the footnote at bottom left of the right column and the logo and page number at bottom right. The title sits top-left inside the navy panel, left-aligned at about 4% margin; right-column text is left-aligned at 53.4% from the left edge.

**Visuals**

Photograph (left column, x 0 to 42.5%, y 40.4 to 100%, full-bleed to the slide edges at left and bottom): a modern glass-and-brick office building seen from a low angle, with stacked rows of tinted windows, a brick band, a white rooftop canopy at upper right and a pale grey tower behind it. A large translucent white cut-out graphic in the form of a stylised rounded shape (a circular or lobed mark) overlays the facade, cropping across the windows at left. In the lower centre a construction site with cranes and scaffolding is visible, with green trees in the foreground at the bottom. Lighting is soft overcast daylight with a hazy, cool atmosphere; dominant colours are pale sky grey, muted teal-green foliage, brick red and blue-grey glass. Composition is a vertical-leaning frame cropped at the slide edges, with no border.
Four circular icons, each a filled navy (#0C2340) circle about 7.1% wide and 12.6% tall, with a white line-art pictogram inside: (1) at top, a scientist figure in a lab coat holding a flask with small bubbles above (about 4.8% by 8.5%, a placed image); (2) a rocket launching diagonally up-right with motion lines below-left; (3) two hands clasping in a handshake. The fourth circle is filled teal (#00857C) and contains a white line-art bar chart of five ascending bars with an upward trend arrow above.
Divider: a thin light grey horizontal hairline (about 1 px, #C8CDD3) running from about x 45.6% to 95.2% at y 60.4%, interrupted at centre by a downward grey chevron (stroke about 3 px, #9EA7B3, about 5% wide and 4.5% tall) at about x 68.3 to 73.2%, y 58.2 to 62.6%.
Logo: a small teal (#00857C) four-lobed knot-shaped mark in the bottom right corner, about 3.1% wide and 6.6% tall.

**Design**

Background is plain white (#FFFFFF) on the right and a deep navy panel (#0C2240) on the upper left. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in a light geometric humanist sans-serif (Invention-Light), regular-light weight, around 5.9% of slide height per line, sentence case, left-aligned, roughly 1.2 line spacing. Row headings are navy (#0C2340) in bold sans-serif (Invention-Bold) at about 3.7% of slide height. Row body text is navy (#0C2340) in Invention Regular at about 2.6% of slide height, with line spacing about 1.3. The conclusion heading is teal (#00857C) in Invention Bold at about 4.4% of slide height; its body is navy (#0C2340) in Invention Regular at about 3.3% of slide height. Footnote is Invention Regular at about 1.7%, navy. Page number is light grey (#9EA7B3) at about 1.3%. Icon circles are flat with no shadow, no gradients, no rounded corners beyond the circle itself. Lines are thin and light grey.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.
- What would change the score: I would split the slide 21 timeline across two pages and trim the cumulative greyed-out lists on slides 6 and 7 so each page shows one clear set of milestones.

**Text**

Achieved notable clinical and regulatory milestones in 2025 / Significant Approvals / Oncology / KEYTRUDA QLEX¹ / KEYTRUDA in earlier-stage cancers / Cisplatin-ineligible MIBC (KEYNOTE-905²) / Resectable LA HNSCC (KEYNOTE-689) / Cardiometabolic / WINREVAIR PAH label update (ZENITH) / Infectious Disease / ENFLONSIA for RSV / Animal Health / BRAVECTO QUANTUM / NUMELVI (EU) / Key Positive Data Readouts / Oncology / KEYTRUDA / PROC (KEYNOTE-B96) / MIBC (KEYNOTE-B15) / WELIREG / Adjuvant RCC (LITESPARK-022) / 2L+ RCC (LITESPARK-011) / Cardiometabolic / Enlicitide (CORALreef Lipids, HeFH, AddOn) / WINREVAIR (HYPERION, CADENCE) / Infectious Disease / MK-8527 for HIV PrEP / Islatravir-based regimens³ for HIV treatment / 1. MK-3475A marketed in the EU as KEYTRUDA SC 2. KEYNOTE-905 was conducted in collaboration with Pfizer and Astellas / 3. Includes islatravir in combination with doravirine and in combination with lenacapavir / 6

**Layout**

Full-width navy title band across the top 18% of the slide with the title left-aligned at about 3% margin. Below it two text columns: a left 'Significant Approvals' column (x 3 to 29%) and a right 'Key Positive Data Readouts' column (x 31.5 to 58%) separated by a dotted vertical rule at about x 30%; the right half of the slide is empty. Footnotes sit bottom-left at y 93.5%, with the logo and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

none. The only graphic is a small teal four-lobed logo mark at bottom right (about x 91.8, y 92.8, 3.1 by 6.6%), with a dotted grey vertical divider line and two short coloured rules above the column headings. No charts, photographs or diagrams.

**Design**

Background white. Title band solid navy #0C2340 with white title text (Invention Light, regular weight, sans-serif). Left column is faded by a 75% white overlay, so its headings appear teal #00857C at reduced strength and its bullets appear light grey (about #BFC5CC); the teal-coloured items remain light teal (about #A9D9D4). Right column: headings and category labels navy #0C2340 bold; sub-items navy #0C2340 regular; highlighted items periwinkle #688CE8 bold on pale lavender #DDDCFA fills. Column heading 'Key Positive Data Readouts' is periwinkle #688CE8. Typeface Invention family (sans-serif), Bold for headings and category labels, Regular for sub-items. Title about 5.1% of slide height; body about 2.6%; footnotes about 1.7%. Thin rules about 0.4% tall, teal above the left heading and periwinkle above the right heading. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Full-slide background fill (Solid white covering the slide, back layer.)

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.
- What would change the score: I would split the slide 21 timeline across two pages and trim the cumulative greyed-out lists on slides 6 and 7 so each page shows one clear set of milestones.

**Layout**

Three-column layout on a white slide beneath a full-width navy title band; the title sits top-left inside the band at about 3% left margin. Column headers align on one baseline at about 24% height, with the left and middle columns at x 3% and 32% and the right column at x 60%, separated by faint dotted vertical dividers. Footnotes are left-aligned at the bottom left, the logo sits bottom right above the page number, which is at the far right bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Left and middle columns (Significant Approvals and Key Positive Data Readouts) are covered by a 75% opaque white overlay that fades their text to pale grey and pale tints. The right column is not covered and shows full-strength navy and blue text. Three light blue (#E1F1FD) highlight rectangles sit behind the highlighted right-column items (sac-TMT at y 32.1, tulisokibart at y 70.4, and the two Ophthalmology items at y 80.5 to 93.7). Column header rules across the top of each column (estimated: teal #B3DDD9 at left, pale lavender #DCE3F8 in the middle, and a thicker blue #1590F3 at right) and faint dotted vertical dividers (estimated light grey #D0D5DD) at about x 30% and x 58.7%. A small teal logo at bottom right (a four-lobed green icon, #00857C).

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a navy #0C2340 title band across the top 18.4%. Title in Invention Light, white #FFFFFF, about 5.1% of slide height. Column headers Invention Bold about 3.3% of height, coloured teal #00857C, lavender #688CE8 and blue #1590F3 (faded by overlay on left and middle). Subheads Invention Bold about 2.6% in navy #0C2340. Items Invention Regular about 2.6% in navy #0C2340, highlighted items Invention Bold in blue #1590F3. Left column highlighted items teal #00857C bold. Footnotes Invention Regular about 1.7%. Sharp rectangles with no corner radii or shadows; highlight rectangles are flat #E1F1FD fills.

**Layout**

Two-column layout below a full-width navy title band occupying the top 18.4% of the slide. The title sits left-aligned at about 3.1% from the left edge, vertically centred in the band. Left column (product logos) spans about x 3% to 39%; right column (bar chart) spans about x 41% to 97%, separated by a dotted vertical grey divider at about x 40%. Each column has a bold teal section heading with a thin teal rule above it, left-aligned to the column edge. The page number sits bottom right at about x 96.4%, y 95.5%, with a small teal logo mark to its left at the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Five product logos stacked in a two-column grid on the left: WINREVAIR (sotatercept-csrk) top-left, Ohtuvayre (ensifentrine) top-right, CAPVAXIVE top-left below, ENFLONSIA (clesrovimab-cfor) right of it, and KEYTRUDA Qlex full-width-ish below. Each is a flat logo image on white. Right side: one bar chart (embedded as an image, 1100 by 679 px) titled Launch product revenue ($ millions). Type: vertical column chart with solid teal bars and dashed teal outline boxes stacked above some bars. Y axis: $0 to $900, labelled at $0 and $900 with tick marks; the axis is linear in $ millions. X axis: quarters Q2 2024, Q3 2024, Q4 2024, Q1 2025, Q2 2025, Q3 2025. Printed total labels above each bar: $70 (Q2 2024), $203 (Q3 2024), $287 (Q4 2024), $458 (Q1 2025), $568 (Q2 2025), $824 (Q3 2025). Solid teal segment values are not printed; estimated: Q2 2024 about $70, Q3 2024 about $200, Q4 2024 about $250, Q1 2025 about $390, Q2 2025 about $465, Q3 2025 about $690. Dashed outlined segment (top of bar) is the OHTUVAYRE sales reported by Verona Pharma, shown as the difference between the solid bar and the printed total; roughly $9 at Q3 2024, about $37 at Q4 2024, about $77 at Q1 2025, about $103 at Q2 2025, about $134 at Q3 2025 (estimates). Legend below the x axis: small dashed teal-outlined rectangle followed by 'OHTUVAYRE sales reported by Verona Pharma'. No highlighted points beyond the dashed segments. Bars are flat with no gradients or shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width navy #0C2340 title band across the top 18.4% of the slide. White background (#FFFFFF) with a full-width navy title band #0C2340. Title in Invention Light (light sans-serif, regular-light weight), white #FFFFFF, roughly 5.1% of slide height. Section headings in Invention Bold, teal #00857C, about 3.7% of slide height. Chart labels and legend in Invention Regular/Bold, navy #0C2340; totals in bold navy. Teal rules #00857C about 0.4% of slide height above each section heading. Vertical divider is a light grey dotted line #C8CDD3. Bars teal #00857C with dashed teal outline #00857C. Page number in light grey #9EA7B3, small. No shadows, no corner radii, no gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width navy title band across the top (about 18% of slide height) with a two-line left-aligned title starting at a 3% left margin. Below, a teal rule and bold teal subtitle sit at the left, followed by three logo-and-caption blocks in a loose staggered arrangement on the left two-thirds: Verona Pharma and OHTUVAYRE top left, Cidara and MK-1406 top centre-right, Hengrui and MK-7262 centred below. The right third of the slide is empty white. The footnote sits bottom left, the page number bottom right beside a small teal logo.

**Visuals**

Three pharmaceutical company logos and one corporate logo. (1) Verona Pharma logo: a crimson #C8102E-like wordmark 'Verona Pharma' with a registered mark, with a navy #1F2A6B starburst (radiating thin lines with a small centre dot and dashes) above the right end of the wordmark; boxed on white, no crop. (2) Cidara Therapeutics logo: a cluster of about twelve filled circles in teal #2A7F9E and burnt orange #D9622B arranged in an irregular dot cluster to the left, followed by the word 'CIDARA' in teal #2A7F9E large sans-serif capitals and 'THERAPEUTICS' below in widely letter-spaced grey #5A5F66 capitals; a superscript '1' sits to its upper right. (3) Hengrui logo: a dark blue #1A4A9A ellipse containing stylised white slanted strokes and a small yellow-orange capsule shape at upper left, with a white notch at upper right; 'HENGRUI' in blue #1A4A9A capitals below. (4) A small teal #00857C four-lobed flower or clover-like mark at bottom right. No photographs, charts or diagrams. A thin teal #00857C horizontal rule spans the top of the content area.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a navy #0C2340 band across the top 18.4% of the slide. Two-tone corporate look: white body with a deep navy #0C2340 header band occupying the top 18.4% of the slide. Title in a light sans-serif (Invention Light style) in white, roughly 5.1% of slide height, single weight. Subtitle and accent rule in teal #00857C, bold sans-serif (Invention Bold style) at about 3.3% of slide height. Drug names in bold navy #0C2340 at about 3.0% of slide height; descriptions in regular navy #0C2340 sans-serif at about 2.6%, centred, with generous line spacing. Footnote and page number are small regular sans-serif (1.7% and 1.3%); page number in grey #9EA7B3. Shapes are plain with square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width navy title band across the top 18.4% of the slide, with the title left-aligned at about 3.1% from the left edge and set on two lines. Below it the body is split into two columns: a left column of three drug-transaction blocks (Verona Pharma, Cidara, Hengrui) and a right column of a logo grid, separated by a dotted vertical divider at about 58.4% of width. Each column has a teal rule across its top at about 21.8% of height with a bold teal heading beneath it. A footnote sits at the bottom left at about 93.5% of height, a small teal company mark at bottom right, and the page number '10' in the bottom-right corner.

**Visuals**

Logos and icons, arranged as in the slide. Left column: Verona Pharma wordmark in magenta with a blue starburst sunburst graphic above its right end; Cidara Therapeutics logo with a cluster of teal, blue and orange-red dots to the left of the CIDARA wordmark in blue-grey and THERAPEUTICS in spaced capitals beneath; Hengrui logo, a dark blue oval with a yellow and white stylised emblem above the HENGRUI wordmark in dark blue. Right column logo grid, row by row: Acceleron Pharma (blue streaks with ACCELERON wordmark in dark navy and PHARMA beneath); Prometheus Biosciences (dark blue wordmark, Biosciences in blue beneath); Daiichi-Sankyo (blue and green circular emblem with Daiichi-Sankyo wordmark in dark blue); EyeBio (pink-purple starburst with EyeBio wordmark in black); Kelun-Biotech (red-and-blue circular emblem with KELUN-BIOTECH in dark blue and Chinese characters); Hansoh Pharma (teal hexagon with yellow emblem and HANSOH wordmark in dark grey); LaNova Medicines (Chinese characters in blue with LaNova Medicines in green); moderna (red lowercase wordmark with blue dashed underline); Curon (dark blue and teal swoosh with CURON in blue); Imago BioSciences (IMAGO in grey, blue dotted-sphere emblem, BIOSCIENCES in grey); Elanco Aqua (blue wordmark with Aqua beneath); Harpoon Therapeutics (dark grey HARPOON with Therapeutics beneath and a blue swoosh); Gilead (red shield-like emblem with GILEAD in grey); Orion (blue arc with ORION in dark blue). Bottom right: small teal-green circular company mark of four lobes. No photographs or charts; all logos are flat, on white.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a dark navy title band #0C2340. Title text white #FFFFFF in Invention Light (sans-serif, regular weight), about 5.1% of slide height. Column headings in Invention Bold, teal #00857C, about 3.3% of slide height. Body and drug names in Invention Regular and Invention Bold, navy #0C2340, body about 2.6% and drug names about 3.0% of slide height. Footnote in Invention Regular, navy, about 1.7%. Page number in Invention Regular, grey #9EA7B3, about 1.3%. Teal horizontal rules #00857C about 0.35% of height thick above each column. Dotted vertical divider in light grey #9EA7B3, about 0.3% wide, dotted. No shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A few large figures side by side, with a single headline sentence stating the comparison, make the growth story readable at a glance.
- Review: The deck has a clear route: milestones, launches, business development, the opportunity sizing on slides 11 to 13, then readouts and priorities.
- Review: The key charts on slides 11 and 13 are clear.
- Worth keeping: Keep the slide 11 bar comparison with its >2X bracket and large figures, the stacked build on slide 13, and the consistent navy title band and logo system.

opportunity since last year
~$35B

total KEYTRUDA sales1,2

**Layout**

Full-width navy title band across the top 18% of the slide with the two-line title left-aligned at a 3% left margin, white below. Below the band, a two-bar column chart occupies the centre-right with a horizontal baseline at about 84% height; the right bar carries a bracket callout on its right, and a footnote line sits along the bottom-left with the logo and page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart with a bracketed comparison, no axes or gridlines. Bar 1 (left): solid navy #0C2340 column labelled ~$35B above it, category label 'Consensus 2028 total KEYTRUDA sales' with superscript 1,2 beneath. Bar 2 (right): stacked column, bottom segment dark teal #008477 and top segment mint #6ECEB2 (the top segment is the new opportunity), total label >$70B above; the mint segment is marked with a mint right-facing brace and the callout '+$20B opportunity since last year'. The bars are about equal in width; the right bar is about 1.5 times the height of the left bar, with values printed and no scale. A navy outlined oval containing '>2X' sits on an elbow connector: a horizontal navy line leaves the oval to the left, turns down to the top of the ~$35B label; a second line leaves the oval to the right, runs to the right and turns down with a filled navy arrowhead pointing at the >$70B label. Horizontal navy baseline with three small downward ticks at the left edge, centre and right end of the category area.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background, with a full-width navy #0C2340 band across the top 18.4%. Clean corporate slide. Background white #FFFFFF; title band navy #0C2340 with title in white #FFFFFF, Invention Light (humanist sans-serif), title size about 5.1% of slide height, single weight. Body and labels navy #0C2340 in Invention Regular, about 3.0% of height for category labels and 3.3% for the callout; values in Invention Bold about 5.2% of height. Accent colours: mint #6ECEB2 for '+$20B' and its segment and brace; teal #008477 for the lower stacked segment and logo. Page number in grey #9EA7B3. Lines are navy with about 0.3% of height stroke, square corners, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck has a clear route: milestones, launches, business development, the opportunity sizing on slides 11 to 13, then readouts and priorities.
- Suggested repair: Fill or rebalance the empty right side of slide 14, raise contrast on the pale labels on slide 19, and enlarge text on the slide 21 timeline and the slide 12 program grid.

**Text**

Ten key programs represent 70% of >$70 billion commercial opportunity and will potentially transform our portfolio / >$70B / WINREVAIR (sotatercept-csrk) for injection 45 mg, 60 mg / MK-1406 / ifinatamab deruxtecan3 / Ohtuvayre (ensifentrine) Inhalation Suspension 3 mg/2.5 mL / islatravir based QW regimens2 / sacituzumab tirumotecan4 / enlicitide decanoate / ENFLONSIA (clesrovimab-cfor) 105 mg injection / tulisokibart / MK-3000 / ~70% / Mid 2030s revenue opportunity from potential new growth drivers1 / Oncology / Cardiometabolic & Respiratory / Infectious Disease / Immunology / Ophthalmology / 1. Non-risk adjusted annual sales by the mid 2030s 2. In collaboration with Gilead 3. In collaboration with Daiichi Sankyo 4. In collaboration with Kelun Biotech / 12

**Layout**

Full-width dark navy title band across the top (about the top 18% of the slide) with a two-line left-aligned title. Below, a left column holds a single stacked bar chart with its caption; the right area is a light grey panel holding a 3-column by 4-row grid of program tiles, with a legend row of five category chips beneath it and a footnote line at the bottom left, with a small logo and page number at bottom right. Left margin about 3% of slide width; slide number sits at the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Stacked bar chart (single column, no axes, no gridlines) with a thin horizontal baseline. Bar top label '>$70B' in bold navy. Bar spans about x 11% to 26% and y 16% to 79% of slide height; the lower teal segment (#008378-like teal) fills roughly the lower three-quarters and the upper segment is dark navy #0C2340; the teal segment is labelled '~70%' in white bold near its centre; no axis units or ranges printed. Caption beneath the bar: 'Mid 2030s revenue opportunity from potential new growth drivers' with superscript 1. A teal-to-transparent gradient wedge connects the top and bottom corners of the bar to the grey panel edges (a zoom/callout effect). Right panel: 12 tiles in a grid with three columns (x about 38%, 56.7%, 75.1%) and four rows (y about 28.9%, 42.8%, 56.7%, 71%) plus MK-3000 in the middle column at y 85%. Tiles colour-coded by category: Cardiometabolic & Respiratory tiles lavender #DDDCFA with a #5350E4 left bar (WINREVAIR logo, Ohtuvayre logo, enlicitide decanoate); Infectious Disease tiles pale blue #E1F1FD with a #69B8F7 left bar (MK-1406, islatravir based QW regimens, ENFLONSIA logo, MK-3000 in yellow); Oncology/Immunology tiles mint #E2F5F0 with #6ECEB2 left bar (ifinatamab deruxtecan, sacituzumab tirumotecan); Immunology tile pale lime #F2FBD6 with #BFED33 left bar (tulisokibart); MK-3000 yellow #FFF9C1 with #FFF063 left bar. Logos: WINREVAIR wordmark in bold navy-blue italic caps with a multicolour swoosh above it and a small grey text line; Ohtuvayre wordmark in navy lowercase with a blue wave over the 'v', with '(ensifentrine) Inhalation Suspension' and a cyan '3 mg/2.5 mL' tag; ENFLONSIA wordmark in dark green spaced capitals with a green leaf-like emblem above and '(clesrovimab-cfor) 105 mg injection' below. Bottom-right small teal two-tone circular logo mark (corporate icon).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F7F7F7 panel behind the tile grid and a navy #0C2340 title band across the top. Clean corporate look: white slide with a navy #0C2340 header band. Title in a light sans-serif (Invention Light style) white, roughly 5.1% of slide height. Body and tile text in Invention Regular navy #0C2340 about 3% of slide height; bar label in Invention Bold. Tile text is regular-weight sans; superscripts are small. Accents are teal #008378 and #6ECEB2, with category colours: lavender #5350E4 and #DDDCFA, pale blue #69B8F7 and #E1F1FD, mint #6ECEB2 and #E2F5F0, lime #BFED33 and #F2FBD6, yellow #FFF063 and #FFF9C1. Tiles are flat rectangles with square corners, no shadows, a thin coloured left edge bar about 0.3% of slide width. Panel background #F7F7F7. Footnote 1.7% of slide height in navy; page number small grey #9EA7B3.

**Layout**

A full-width navy header band fills the top 18.4% of the slide and holds a two-line left-aligned title at 3.1% left margin. Below, a left column holds a two-bar chart (a single consensus bar and a stacked bar) with captions beneath, and a right column holds the pipeline heading, three rows of program names separated by dashed rules, and a row of four product logos/wordmarks. Footnotes sit bottom-left across the full width at about 91.7% to 97% height, with a small corner logo at bottom right and the page number '13' at bottom right beside it.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked bar chart drawn as an image with transparent background. Left bar is a single solid navy #0C2340 column, x about 9.5% to 24.5% of slide width, top at about 50.2% and base at about 75.8% of slide height, labelled above as ~$35B, captioned Consensus 2028 total KEYTRUDA sales (superscript 1,2). Right bar is a stacked column, x about 35.1% to 50.2%, top at about 28.1% and base at about 75.8%, labelled above as >$70B. Its segments from bottom to top are: Today, teal #00857C, about 62.5% to 75.8% height; 2026, mint #6ECEB2, about 51.5% to 62.5%; 2027, periwinkle blue #688CE8, about 34.9% to 51.5%; 2028+, light sky blue #69B8F7, about 28.1% to 34.9%. Segment labels are white bold text centred in each segment. Caption below reads Mid 2030s revenue opportunity from potential new growth drivers (superscript 3). Dashed grey #BFBFBF line runs horizontally from the top of the navy bar to the stacked bar at about 50.3% height. Axis: a thin navy #0C2340 horizontal line from about x 4.2% to 55.5% at about 75.8% height, with small vertical tick marks at x 4.2%, 29.8% and 55.5%. No numeric axis, no y-axis, no legend (segments are labelled directly). Arrow: a solid light grey #D9D9D9 upward block arrow at x 50.7% to 57.1%, y 26.2% to 75.5%, with a rotated navy bold label reading Increasingly clinically derisked (superscript 4) running upward inside its shaft. Pipeline: three rows of names in colour (#688CE8 row 1, #6ECEB2 row 2) with superscript footnote numbers, and enlicitide decanoate in #00857C. Logos (left to right): WINREVAIR (sotatercept-csrk) for injection 45 mg, 60 mg wordmark in navy and purple-blue with a small red-blue swoosh; Ohtuvayre (ensifentrine) inhalation suspension, 3 mg/2.5 mL, navy wordmark with blue swoosh and a small light blue label; ENFLONSIA (clesrovimab-cfor) 105 mg injection, dark green wordmark with a green leaf icon above; enlicitide decanoate in bold teal text. Corner logo: small dark teal four-lobed mark at bottom right, about 91.8% to 94.9% x and 92.8% to 99.4% y. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background with a full-width navy #0C2340 header band at the top (0% to 18.4% of height). Clean corporate sans-serif look on a white ground with a dark navy header band. Title is white Invention Light, about 5.1% of slide height per line, two lines. Body and captions are navy #0C2340 Invention Regular about 2.6% of slide height; footnotes are Invention Regular about 1.7%. Bold Invention is used for chart totals (about 3.7%) and pipeline names (about 3.0%). Accent colours: light blue #69B8F7 for the pipeline heading, periwinkle #688CE8 for the first pipeline row, mint #6ECEB2 for the second row, dark teal #00857C for the stacked bar base and enlicitide decanoate. Lines are thin dashed grey #BFBFBF (about 0.2% stroke) for pipeline row separators and the bar connector, and a thin navy axis line. No rounded corners, no shadows, no gradients, no textures.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A structured table of mechanism, therapeutic area, disease area and readout timing turns a dense pipeline into a scannable timeline.
- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.
- Suggested repair: Fill or rebalance the empty right side of slide 14, raise contrast on the pale labels on slide 19, and enlarge text on the slide 21 timeline and the slide 12 program grid.

Row labels: Mechanism of action; Therapeutic area; Disease area; Phase 3 study PCD. Cell values: ISL / LEN1 mechanism Nucleoside reverse transcriptase translocation inhibitor / capsid inhibitor; therapeutic area HIV; disease area Treatment (oral, QW); study ISLEND-1 and ISLEND-2 Apr 2026. MK-3000 mechanism Wnt agonist; area Ophthalmology; disease Diabetic macular edema; study BRUNELLO Sept 2026 (BAROLO Mar 2027). tulisokibart mechanism TL1A mAb; area Immunology; disease Ulcerative colitis; study ATLAS-UC Aug 2026. sac-TMT2 mechanism TROP2 ADC; area Oncology; disease Various tumor types; study TroFuse (16 Phase 3 studies; PCDs beginning in 2027). MK-1406 mechanism zanamivir-Fc conjugate; area Infectious Disease; disease Influenza; study ANCHOR Jan 2027. I-DXd3 mechanism B7H3 ADC; area Oncology; disease Various tumor types; study IDeate (3 Phase 3 studies; PCDs beginning in 2027). Small teal logo mark bottom-right: a four-lobed rounded teal emblem, about 3% wide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate table style. Title band navy #0C2340 with white light sans-serif title. Body text navy #0C2340. Row label cells navy #0C2340 with white bold sans-serif labels. Data cells light grey #F7F7F7 with 0.5% white gutters. 2026 header mint #6ECEB2 with navy/white text; 2027 header periwinkle #688CE8. Typeface Invention (Invention-Light for title, Invention-Bold for headers and labels, Invention-Regular for body), a humanist sans-serif. Title size about 5.1% of slide height; headers and labels about 3.0%; body cells about 2.6%; footnotes 1.7%. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 17

**Text**

Q&A / Rob Davis / Chairman and Chief Executive Officer / Dr. Dean Li / President, Merck Research Laboratories

**Layout**

Two-column split with a white left column and a photograph filling the right half. The logo sits at the top-left corner (about 1.1% from the left and 2.1% from the top). The 'Q&A' title sits left-aligned at 3.1% from the left, around 42% down the slide. Two speaker rows (headshot with name and title) sit in the lower left, starting at 58.7% and 74.6% down. The photo runs from the vertical centre of the slide's left edge at 50% to the right edge, from the top edge down to 88.9% of the height, leaving a white band along the bottom 11.1%. There is no footer, slide number or source line.

**Visuals**

1) Logo: a teal four-lobed mark made of rounded petals with a horizontal white split through the middle, in #00857C, at the top-left corner. 2) Photograph (right half, x 50 to 100%, y 0 to 88.9%, hard-edged rectangle, full-bleed to the top and right edges): an audience in a bright, blurred lobby with several hands raised. In the foreground, a woman with dense curly reddish-brown hair faces away from the camera, and her right arm is raised with a dark patterned blue long-sleeved shirt, a wooden bead bracelet and hoop earrings. Other raised hands, a woman in a black top and a man in a white shirt are blurred in the background. Ceiling lights appear as soft bokeh strings near the top. Composition is shallow depth of field with the subject in the lower-centre; lighting is soft, warm and natural; dominant colours are warm beige, brown, cream and deep navy. 3) Headshot of Rob Davis (x 3.1%, y 58.7%, 8.0% by 14.3%, boxed square-cornered rectangle): a smiling bald man with rectangular dark-framed glasses, navy suit, white shirt and purple tie, against a mid-grey studio backdrop, head and shoulders framed, lit evenly from the front. 4) Headshot of Dr. Dean Li (x 3.1%, y 74.6%, 8.1% by 14.3%, boxed square-cornered rectangle): a smiling man with short hair, dark-framed glasses, light grey suit, white shirt and patterned tie, against a mid-grey studio backdrop, head and shoulders framed, lit evenly from the front. 5) Grey panel #D9D9D9 (x 50%, y 0%, 50% by 88.9%) sits directly behind the photograph and is hidden by it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide. Clean corporate look. White background (#FFFFFF). Title 'Q&A' is set in a light, geometric sans-serif (Invention-Light style) in navy #0C2340, large at about 7.9% of slide height (cap height about 5.5% of height). Speaker names are set in the same sans family, shown visibly heavier than the title, in navy #0C2340, at about 3.0% of slide height. Speaker titles are set in light weight navy #0C2340 at about 3.0% of slide height. The only accent colour is the teal logo #00857C. Lines and shapes: none; photographs are square-cornered rectangles with no border, radius, shadow or gradient.

### Slide 19

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A prior-versus-updated comparison by therapeutic area shows how the opportunity changed without needing extra explanation.
- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.
- Suggested repair: Fill or rebalance the empty right side of slide 14, raise contrast on the pale labels on slide 19, and enlarge text on the slide 21 timeline and the slide 12 program grid.

**Text**

Strategic business development drives increase in mid-2030s commercial opportunity / Prior Opportunity1 / Updated Opportunity1 / Oncology / >$25B / >$25B / Cardiometabolic & Respiratory / ~$15B / ~$20B2 / Infectious Disease / >$5B / ~$15B3 / Immunology / >$5B / >$5B / Ophthalmology / Multibillion / >$5B / Amounts above exclude currently marketed products (except WINREVAIR, OHTUVAYRE, CAPVAXIVE and ENFLONSIA), early-phase programs and additional business development / 1. Non-risk adjusted annual sales by the mid 2030s 2. Now includes OHTUVAYRE 3. Now includes MK-1406, ENFLONSIA, V181 / 19

**Layout**

Five-row comparison grid on a white slide. A full-width navy title band sits across the top with the two-line title left-aligned at the top-left margin; column headers Prior Opportunity and Updated Opportunity sit above the second and fourth columns. Five rows of coloured category label bars (left column, x 3.1 to 29.1), grey prior-value cells (x 32.5 to 52.0), arrow images (x 55.4 to 72.9) and pale updated-value cells (x 76.3 to 95.8) run down the slide. A footnote line and a numbered footnote sit at the bottom left, with a small logo and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Comparison table-style diagram with five rows. Row 1 Oncology: label bar teal #00857C with white bold text; prior value >$25B in grey #7F7F7F on #F2F2F2 cell; a horizontal arrow fading from white at its tail to teal #00857C at its head pointing right; updated value >$25B in teal #00857C on pale mint #E2F5F0 cell. Row 2 Cardiometabolic & Respiratory: label bar indigo #5350E4 with white bold two-line text; prior ~$15B grey on #F2F2F2; arrow fading from white to indigo #5350E4; updated ~$20B with superscript 2 in indigo #5350E4 on pale lavender #DDDCFA. Row 3 Infectious Disease: label bar sky blue #69B8F7 with white bold text; prior >$5B grey on #F2F2F2; arrow fading from white to sky blue #69B8F7; updated ~$15B with superscript 3 in sky blue #69B8F7 on pale blue #E1F1FD. Row 4 Immunology: label bar lime #BFED33 with white bold text; prior >$5B grey on #F2F2F2; arrow fading from white to lime #BFED33; updated >$5B in olive-green #9AC612 on pale lime #F2FBD6. Row 5 Ophthalmology: label bar yellow #FFF063 with white bold text; prior Multibillion grey on #F2F2F2; arrow fading from white to yellow #FFF063; updated >$5B in dark gold #B1A000 on pale yellow #FFFCE0. Dotted light grey row separators run between rows. Small teal logo mark at bottom right (a rounded teal pill-and-circle emblem, two stacked teal shapes, colour #00857C), no other logos or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a navy #0C2340 title band across the top. White background with a dark navy #0C2340 title band across the top about 18 percent of slide height. Title text is white Invention Light, a humanist sans-serif, regular weight, about 5.1 percent of slide height. Column headers are Invention Bold about 3.7 percent: Prior Opportunity in grey #7F7F7F, Updated Opportunity in navy #0C2340. Body values are Invention Bold about 4.1 percent of slide height, colour-coded per row. Category labels are Invention Bold about 3.7 percent in white. Footnotes are Invention Regular about 2.0 and 1.7 percent in navy #0C2340. Page number is Invention Regular about 1.3 percent in grey #9EA7B3. Shapes are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows; arrows are flat right-pointing block arrows with gradient fills; separators are fine dotted grey lines.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.

**Text**

Robust pipeline to drive patient impact and value creation into the mid-2030s / Updated Opportunity1 / Additional Opportunities / Oncology / >$25B / Cardiometabolic & Respiratory / ~$20B / Infectious Disease / ~$15B / Immunology / >$5B / Ophthalmology / >$5B / Animal Health / Expect revenue to more than double by the mid-2030s2 / Early-Phase Programs / Many advancing to Phase 2 in near-term / Business Development / Significant capacity to further augment pipeline / Amounts above exclude currently marketed products (except WINREVAIR, OHTUVAYRE, CAPVAXIVE and ENFLONSIA), early-phase programs and additional business development / 1. Non-risk adjusted annual sales by the mid 2030s 2. Expected to double off of 2024 baseline revenue / 20

**Layout**

A full-width navy title band occupies the top 18.4% of the slide, with the white title left-aligned at x 3.1% and y 4.0%. Below it the body is two columns: on the left, a grid of five coloured label boxes (x 3.1 to 29.1%) paired with five pale value boxes (x 31.0 to 50.5%) under a bold 'Updated Opportunity' heading; on the right, a light grey panel (x 60.0 to 96.9%, y 26.4 to 89.3%) holding three stacked sections under the heading 'Additional Opportunities', with a large navy plus sign bridging the two columns at centre. A grey footnote line sits left-aligned at y 91% beneath the grid, then a small footnote at y 94.9%, a teal logo at bottom right and the page number at far right.

**Visuals**

Five coloured horizontal label boxes stacked down the left, each with a centred white bold label: Oncology (teal), Cardiometabolic & Respiratory (violet), Infectious Disease (sky blue), Immunology (lime), Ophthalmology (yellow). Beside each sits a pale tinted value box with a centred bold value in the label colour: >$25B (teal on mint), ~$20B (violet on lavender), ~$15B (sky blue on pale blue), >$5B (lime-green on pale lime), >$5B (olive-gold on pale yellow). Thin light grey dotted separators run between rows. A large navy plus sign sits between the two columns, its vertical bar centred near x 55% and its horizontal bar spanning about x 49.8 to 60.8%, with a small white notch cut at its left edge beside the ~$15B value box. The right grey panel contains three navy horizontal rules and three line icons: a navy line drawing of a dog and a cat at about x 61 to 68%, y 33 to 45%, beside Animal Health; a navy map pin with a rising arrow line joined by three small circles at about x 61 to 69%, y 55 to 66%, beside Early-Phase Programs; and a navy lightbulb with a double chevron inside and radiating rays at about x 61 to 67%, y 72 to 85%, beside Business Development. A teal four-lobed corporate logo sits at bottom right (x 91.8, y 92.8%). No chart or table is present.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: slide 14 leaves the right third empty, slides 6 and 7 grey out earlier columns while stacking small text, slide 19 uses pale text on pale fills (yellow, light blue), and slides 20 and 21 have very small labels that will not read in a room.
- Suggested repair: Fill or rebalance the empty right side of slide 14, raise contrast on the pale labels on slide 19, and enlarge text on the slide 21 timeline and the slide 12 program grid.
- What would change the score: I would split the slide 21 timeline across two pages and trim the cumulative greyed-out lists on slides 6 and 7 so each page shows one clear set of milestones.

**Layout**

Full-width navy title band across the top (about 18% of slide height) with a two-line left-aligned title starting at about 3% from the left edge. Below it, five equal-width gray chevron headers (2024, 2025, 2026, 2027, 2028+) run left to right as a timeline, with stacked drug cards under each year; a legend sits lower left, a dotted divider runs above a bottom table of therapeutic areas and commercial opportunity, and a footnote block with the Merck-style logo and page number 21 sits at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Timeline diagram with five gray chevron arrows labelled 2024, 2025, 2026, 2027 and 2028+ across the top. Under each year are rounded rectangular cards, each with a coloured border and left accent bar keyed to therapeutic area: Winrevair, Ohtuvayre and Capvaxive logos in 2024; Keytruda Qlex, Enflonsia, enlicitide decanoate and doravirine + islatravir in 2025; islatravir + lenacapavir, MK-3000 and tulisokibart in 2026; MK-1406, sacituzumab tirumotecan, ifinatamab deruxtecan, nemtabrutinib, MK-8527, zilovertamab vedotin, raludotatug deruxtecan and bomedemstat in 2027; intismeran autogene, opevesostat, calderasib, patritumab deruxtecan, V181, gocatamig, MK-1045, MK-8748 and islatravir + ulonivirine in 2028+. Nine cards carry a navy filled five-point star at their right edge (Winrevair, Ohtuvayre, Enflonsia, enlicitide decanoate, islatravir + lenacapavir, MK-3000, tulisokibart, sacituzumab tirumotecan, ifinatamab deruxtecan). Legend: navy star = Key near-term programs; grey vertical bar = First-in-class potential. Bottom table: header row of five colour-coded boxes (Oncology mint, Cardiometabolic & Respiratory lavender, Infectious Disease light blue, Immunology pale green, Ophthalmology pale yellow) and a value row of five light grey cells reading >$25B¹, ~$20B, ~$15B, >$5B, >$5B. Logos: WINREVAIR, Ohtuvayre, CAPVAXIVE, KEYTRUDA Qlex, ENFLONSIA, and a small teal Merck-style mark bottom right. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a navy #0C2340 header band across the top 18.4% of the slide. White slide background with a dark navy header band. Title in white light-weight geometric humanist sans-serif (Invention Light style), body and card text in navy #0C2340 with bold weight for drug names and regular weight for descriptors. Accents: teal #00857C, lavender-purple #5350E4, light blue #69B8F7, yellow #FFF063, lime #9AC612, navy #0C2340. Chevrons light grey #D9D9D9 with navy year labels. Cards have 1 pt-style thin coloured borders, square-ish corners with slight rounding, no shadows, no gradients. Title about 5.1% of slide height; body text about 2.0% of slide height; footnotes about 1.7%.
